Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any spin module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 324. The projective resolution has length at most 18 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 324 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the spin structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 18. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the spin space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 733 and the convergence rate is O(400/n^4).
